3302    ///   Detcore renders in this session (`ProcfsFile::rendered_path`),
3303    ///   `smaps_rollup` among them only while the backend's memory map of the
3304    ///   reader's address space is complete, without which its read refuses
3305    ///   (`smaps::smaps_rollup_refusal`, dh-62azc), the
3306    ///   `fd` directory it serves (`descriptor_directory`: a process's to
3307    ///   every thread of it, a thread's to that thread alone, which is the
3308    ///   reader taking the listing), and a process's `task`, which it serves
3309    ///   too; in another process's directory, not its `fd` directory, which
3310    ///   Detcore refuses by name there (`other_process_procfs`, until package
3311    ///   K item 2b slice 2b-5 serves it), while its `cmdline` and `environ`
3312    ///   are listed (slice 2b-3 serves them from the strings its `execve`
3313    ///   passed);
3314    /// - in `task`, every thread of the process.
3315    ///
3316    /// Every other name in Linux's table is left out, deliberately: on this
3317    /// backend its open reaches the KVM provider's procfs, which answers
3318    /// `status` and `maps` from its own minimal renderings, not Linux's, and
3319    /// refuses the rest (`EACCES`), which no listing should advertise. An
3320    /// open of such a name by its spelling is answered as before. Each entry
3321    /// is numbered by its path's key, as the files Detcore renders are, and
3322    /// the stream keeps Linux's order (`DirectoryOrder::ProcessEntries`, the
3323    /// threads in `task` ascending).
3324    async fn task_placeholder_snapshot<G: Guest<Self>>(
3325        &self,
3326        guest: &mut G,
3327        binding: &TaskDirectoryBinding,
3328    ) -> Result<(Vec<DirEntry>, DirectoryInodes, DirectoryOrder), Error> {
3329        use crate::procfs::listing;
3330        let directory = binding.modeled();
3331        let facts = crate::tool_global::procfs_listing(
3332            guest,
3333            Some(crate::types::DetPid::from_raw(binding.pid)),
3334        )
3335        .await;
3336        let names = listing::entries(&directory, self.cfg.proc_level, &facts).unwrap_or_default();
3337        let within = binding.within();
3338        let proc = Path::new("/proc");
3339        let session_files = self.rendered_files(guest);
3340        // A thread's descriptor directory Detcore serves to that thread
3341        // alone (`fd_directory::classify`).
3342        let reader = guest.thread_state().dettid.as_raw();
3343        let others = binding.pid != self.own_detpid(guest).as_raw();
3344        // `smaps_rollup` only where its read can render it: the backend's
3345        // memory map of the reader's address space, the one the read renders,
3346        // is complete (`smaps::smaps_rollup_refusal`, the read's own rule).
3347        // On KVM today it is never complete
3348        // (https://github.com/rrnewton/reverie/issues/1211), so the name
3349        // comes back once the backend reports a complete map (dh-62azc).
3350        let rollup_readable = guest.memory_map(guest.tid()).is_ok_and(|map| {
3351            crate::procfs::memory_map::smaps::smaps_rollup_refusal(&map).is_none()
3352        });
3353        let kept = |name: &[u8]| match name {
3354            b"." | b".." => true,
3355            _ if binding.kind == TaskDirectoryKind::Tasks => true,
3356            // Another process's `cmdline` and `environ` are served from the
3357            // strings its `execve` passed (package K item 2b slice 2b-3,
3358            // `Detcore::serves_exec_strings`), so they are listed as Linux
3359            // lists them, below.
3360            b"fd" if others => false,
3361            b"fd" => binding.kind == TaskDirectoryKind::Process || binding.tid == reader,
3362            b"task" => binding.kind == TaskDirectoryKind::Process,
3363            b"smaps_rollup" if !rollup_readable => false,
3364            name => {
3365                let path = proc
3366                    .join(within.strip_prefix("/").unwrap_or(&within))
3367                    .join(OsStr::from_bytes(name));
3368                crate::procfs::ProcfsFile::from_path(&path).is_some_and(|file| {
3369                    file.per_process_name().is_some()
3370                        && !self.kernel_serves_every_comm(&file)
3371                        && !self.kernel_serves_every_status(&file)
3372                        && !self.kernel_serves_every_environ(&file)
3373                        && file.rendered_path(session_files).is_some()
3374                })
3375            }
3376        };
3377        let entries = names
3378            .into_iter()
3379            .filter(|(name, _)| kept(name))
3380            .map(|(name, ty)| {
3381                let entry = match name.as_slice() {
3382                    b"." => within.clone(),
3383                    b".." => within.parent().unwrap_or(&within).to_path_buf(),
3384                    name => within.join(OsStr::from_bytes(name)),
3385                };
3386                DirEntry {
3387                    ino: procfs_inode::path_inode(binding.device, &entry).ino,
3388                    off: 0,
3389                    ty,
3390                    name,
3391                }
3392            })
3393            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
3394        let inodes = DirectoryInodes::new(binding.device, Some(ProcfsDirectory::new(within)));
3395        let order = match binding.kind {
3396            TaskDirectoryKind::Process => DirectoryOrder::ProcessEntries { thread: false },
3397            TaskDirectoryKind::Thread => DirectoryOrder::ProcessEntries { thread: true },
3398            TaskDirectoryKind::Tasks => DirectoryOrder::ProcfsIds,
3399        };
3400        Ok((entries, inodes, order))
3401    }
3402
3403    /// Answer a `getdents` or `getdents64` of the `/proc` placeholder, or of
3404    /// one of the caller's own task directories (package K slice K10), open
3405    /// as `fd` from its directory stream, shared by the description's
3406    /// aliases: the stream's snapshot of [`Self::root_placeholder_snapshot`]
3407    /// or [`Self::task_placeholder_snapshot`], taken at the first read and at
3408    /// the first after a seek to 0, as for every directory Detcore lists
3409    /// (`DirectoryStream`); a task directory whose task is gone answers
3410    /// `ENOENT` at every call. The kernel position stays at the tag
3411    /// (`Detcore::move_directory_kernel_position` moves no placeholder). A
3412    /// count that does not fit an `int` fits nothing: `EINVAL`, or 0 at the
3413    /// end.
3414    async fn serve_root_placeholder<G: Guest<Self>>(
3415        &self,
3416        guest: &mut G,
3417        call: &Syscall,
3418        fd: RawFd,
3419        state: PlaceholderState,
3420    ) -> Result<i64, Error> {
3421        if state.path_only {
3422            return Err(Errno::EBADF.into());
3423        }
3424        let (buf, capacity, format) = match call {
3425            // x86_64 only: aarch64 has Getdents64 alone.
3426            #[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
3427            Syscall::Getdents(c) => (
3428                c.dirent().map(|dirent| dirent.cast::<u8>()),
3429                c.count() as usize,
3430                DirentFormat::Legacy,
3431            ),
3432            Syscall::Getdents64(c) => (
3433                c.dirent().map(|dirent| dirent.cast::<u8>()),
3434                c.count() as usize,
3435                DirentFormat::Dirent64,
3436            ),
3437            _ => return Err(Errno::ENOSYS.into()),
3438        };
3439        let lock = guest
3440            .thread_state()
3441            .with_detfd(fd, |detfd| detfd.directory_lock())?;
3442        let _serving = lock.lock().await;
3443        // A task directory whose task is gone (or whose number another task
3444        // holds now) lists nothing: Linux's readdir looks the task up first,
3445        // at every call, a stream it began reading or rewound included
3446        // (`proc_pident_readdir`'s `get_proc_task`, `proc_task_readdir`'s
3447        // `proc_inode_is_dead`, fs/proc/base.c at v6.17).
3448        if let PlaceholderDirectory::Task(binding) = &state.directory
3449            && !Self::bound_own_task(guest, binding).await.1
3450        {
3451            return Err(Errno::ENOENT.into());
3452        }
3453        if guest
3454            .thread_state()
3455            .with_detfd(fd, |detfd| detfd.directory_needs_snapshot())?
3456        {
3457            let mark = crate::tool_global::directory_snapshot_mark(guest).await;
3458            let (entries, inodes, order) = match &state.directory {
3459                PlaceholderDirectory::Task(binding) => {
3460                    self.task_placeholder_snapshot(guest, binding).await?
3461                }
3462                PlaceholderDirectory::System(directory) => {
3463                    self.system_placeholder_snapshot(guest, directory)?
3464                }
3465                _ => self.root_placeholder_snapshot(guest, fd).await?,
3466            };
3467            let mut entries = Some(entries);
3468            guest.thread_state().with_detfd(fd, |detfd| {
3469                detfd.install_directory_snapshot(
3470                    entries.take().unwrap_or_default(),
3471                    mark,
3472                    inodes.clone(),
3473                    order,
3474                )
3475            })?;
3476        }
3477        let fits = i32::try_from(capacity).is_ok();
3478        let batch = guest.thread_state().with_detfd(fd, |detfd| {
3479            detfd.with_directory_stream(|stream| {
3480                stream.next_batch(format, if fits { capacity } else { 0 })
3481            })
3482        })??;
3483        match batch {
3484            Ok(batch) if batch.is_empty() => return Ok(0),
3485            Ok(_) if !fits => return Err(Errno::EINVAL.into()),
3486            Ok(_) => {}
3487            Err(errno) => return Err(errno.into()),
3488        }
3489        let Some(buf) = buf else {
3490            return Err(Errno::EFAULT.into());
3491        };
3492        let (sysno, _) = call.into_parts();
3493        let empty = Syscall::from_raw(
3494            sysno,
3495            super::procfs_levels::raw_args([fd as usize, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]),
3496        );
3497        self.serve_next_batch(
3498            guest,
3499            GetdentsCall {
3500                call: *call,
3501                empty,
3502                fd,
3503                buf,
3504                capacity,
3505                format,
3506            },
3507        )
3508        .await
3509    }
3510
3511    /// The metadata of a descriptor directory bound to `binding`, as Linux's
3512    /// `proc_fd_getattr` reports it: a directory of mode 0500
3513    /// (`S_IRUSR|S_IXUSR`, `tgid_base_stuff`), 2 links
3514    /// (`proc_pident_instantiate`), its task's owner (`task_dump_owner`, as for
3515    /// the files Detcore renders), procfs's 1024-byte blocks and device, the
3516    /// inode number Detcore's pool gives its path, and a size that is the
3517    /// number of open descriptors (`proc_readfd_count`).
3518    async fn descriptor_directory_metadata<G: Guest<Self>>(
3519        &self,
3520        guest: &mut G,
3521        binding: &DescriptorDirectoryBinding,
3522        object: Option<RawFd>,
3523    ) -> Result<DetStat, Error> {
3524        let open = match self
3525            .descriptor_directory_table(guest, binding, object)
3526            .await
3527        {
3528            Ok(table) => table.map_or(0, |(numbers, _)| numbers.len()),
3529            Err(Errno::ENOENT) => return Err(Errno::ENOENT.into()),
3530            Err(_) => 0,
3531        };
3532        let owner_path = match binding.table {
3533            DescriptorDirectoryTable::Process => {
3534                PathBuf::from(format!("/proc/{}/stat", binding.pid))
3535            }
3536            DescriptorDirectoryTable::Thread => {
3537                PathBuf::from(format!("/proc/{}/task/{}/stat", binding.pid, binding.tid))
3538            }
3539        };
3540        let owner = self.rendered_file_owner(guest, &owner_path).await?;
3541        let mark = crate::tool_global::directory_snapshot_mark(guest).await;
3542        let ino = self
3543            .directory_inode(guest, binding.device, &binding.within(), mark)
3544            .await;
3545        let epoch = super::rendered_procfs::epoch_time(guest.config().epoch);
3546        Ok(DetStat {
3547            mask: StatxMask::STATX_BASIC_STATS | StatxMask::STATX_MNT_ID,
3548            mode: libc::S_IFDIR | 0o500,
3549            uid: owner.0,
3550            gid: owner.1,
3551            attributes: StatxAttributes::empty(),
3552            attributes_mask: StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_AUTOMOUNT
3553                | StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_MOUNT_ROOT
3554                | StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_DAX,
3555            dev: Self::reported_procfs_device(guest),
3556            rdev: 0,
3557            inode: ino,
3558            nlink: 2,
3559            size: open as i64,
3560            blksize: procfs_inode::PROC_BLOCK_SIZE,
3561            blocks: 0,
3562            atime: epoch,
3563            btime: epoch,
3564            ctime: epoch,
3565            mtime: epoch,
3566        })
3567    }
3568
3569    /// The metadata of `/proc` itself, as Linux's `proc_root_getattr`
3570    /// reports it with Detcore's rules for procfs: a directory with the mode
3571    /// and owner the guest's procfs root has (0555, global root's, which the
3572    /// guest's user namespace does not map), one link (Detcore's number for
3573    /// procfs's root, whose own counts the host's processes), size 0,
3574    /// procfs's 1024-byte blocks and device, the mount root attribute, the
3575    /// epoch, and the inode number its `.` entry gets in a listing of it
3576    /// ([`Self::root_placeholder_snapshot`]): the number the kernel's procfs
3577    /// gives its root (1) where the backend's `/proc` is the kernel's, else
3578    /// its path's key.
3579    async fn root_placeholder_metadata<G: Guest<Self>>(
3580        &self,
3581        guest: &mut G,
3582    ) -> Result<DetStat, Error> {
3583        let (mode, uid, gid) = super::rendered_procfs::guest_procfs_root()?;
3584        let device = Self::procfs_key_device(guest);
3585        let within = PathBuf::from("/");
3586        let raw = if procfs_inode::tool_can_name_guest_descriptors(&self.cfg.backend) {
3587            procfs_inode::PROC_ROOT_INO
3588        } else {
3589            procfs_inode::path_inode(device, &within).ino
3590        };
3591        let inodes = DirectoryInodes::new(device, Some(ProcfsDirectory::new(within)));
3592        let (host, name) = inodes.entry_inode(b".", raw);
3593        let mark = crate::tool_global::directory_snapshot_mark(guest).await;
3594        let ino = crate::tool_global::determinize_listed_inode(guest, host, name, mark)
3595            .await
3596            .0
3597            .as_raw();
3598        let epoch = super::rendered_procfs::epoch_time(guest.config().epoch);
3599        Ok(DetStat {
3600            mask: StatxMask::STATX_BASIC_STATS | StatxMask::STATX_MNT_ID,
3601            mode: libc::S_IFDIR | (mode & 0o7777),
3602            uid,
3603            gid,
3604            attributes: StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_MOUNT_ROOT,
3605            attributes_mask: StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_AUTOMOUNT
3606                | StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_MOUNT_ROOT
3607                | StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_DAX,
3608            dev: Self::reported_procfs_device(guest),
3609            rdev: 0,
3610            inode: ino,
3611            nlink: 1,
3612            size: 0,
3613            blksize: procfs_inode::PROC_BLOCK_SIZE,
3614            blocks: 0,
3615            atime: epoch,
3616            btime: epoch,
3617            ctime: epoch,
3618            mtime: epoch,
3619        })
3620    }
3621
3622    /// The metadata of a placeholder directory: `/proc`'s, or a descriptor
3623    /// directory's, read through the placeholder descriptor `object` where
3624    /// one names it ([`Self::kernel_listing_base`]).
3625    pub(super) async fn placeholder_metadata<G: Guest<Self>>(
3626        &self,
3627        guest: &mut G,
3628        directory: &PlaceholderDirectory,
3629        object: Option<RawFd>,
3630    ) -> Result<DetStat, Error> {
3631        match directory {
3632            PlaceholderDirectory::Root => self.root_placeholder_metadata(guest).await,
3633            PlaceholderDirectory::Descriptors(binding) => {
3634                self.descriptor_directory_metadata(guest, binding, object)
3635                    .await
3636            }
3637            PlaceholderDirectory::Task(binding) => {
3638                self.task_directory_metadata(guest, binding).await
3639            }
3640            PlaceholderDirectory::System(directory) => {
3641                self.system_directory_metadata(guest, directory).await
3642            }
3643        }
3644    }
3645
3646    /// The metadata of the caller's own task directory `binding` (package K
3647    /// slice K10), as Linux reports it (fs/proc/base.c at v6.17): a directory
3648    /// of mode 0555 (`S_IFDIR|S_IRUGO|S_IXUGO`: `proc_pid_instantiate`,
3649    /// `proc_task_instantiate`, the `task` entry of `tgid_base_stuff`); its
3650    /// links, 2 and one per directory in its table (`pid_entry_nlink`, 9 for a
3651    /// process: `task`, `fd`, `map_files`, `fdinfo`, `ns`, `net` and `attr`;
3652    /// 7 for a thread, without `task` and `map_files`), and for `task` 2 and
3653    /// one per thread (`proc_pident_instantiate`'s 2, `proc_task_getattr`);
3654    /// the task's effective owner, whatever its dumpable state, as
3655    /// `pid_getattr` takes it through `task_dump_owner` for exactly this mode
3656    /// (Detcore's virtual root, which owns every task's files it renders,
3657    /// `rendered_file_owner`); size 0; procfs's 1024-byte blocks and device;
3658    /// the inode number Detcore's pool gives its path; and the epoch. Neither
3659    /// getattr looks the task up to fail: a held directory whose task is gone
3660    /// (or whose number another task holds now) still answers, with the
3661    /// links its inode was instantiated with, `task` with 2 and no thread
3662    /// (`proc_task_getattr`), and a process's or a thread's directory owned
3663    /// by global root as the guest's user namespace sees it, which is the
3664    /// owner of `/proc` itself (`pid_getattr`, `guest_procfs_root`); `task`
3665    /// keeps its inode's owner.
3666    async fn task_directory_metadata<G: Guest<Self>>(
3667        &self,
3668        guest: &mut G,
3669        binding: &TaskDirectoryBinding,
3670    ) -> Result<DetStat, Error> {
3671        let (_, current) = Self::bound_own_task(guest, binding).await;
3672        let nlink = match binding.kind {
3673            TaskDirectoryKind::Process => 9,
3674            TaskDirectoryKind::Thread => 7,
3675            TaskDirectoryKind::Tasks if !current => 2,
3676            TaskDirectoryKind::Tasks => {
3677                let facts = crate::tool_global::procfs_listing(
3678                    guest,
3679                    Some(crate::types::DetPid::from_raw(binding.pid)),
3680                )
3681                .await;
3682                2 + facts.threads.len() as u64
3683            }
3684        };
3685        let (uid, gid) = if current || binding.kind == TaskDirectoryKind::Tasks {
3686            (0, 0)
3687        } else {
3688            let (_, uid, gid) = super::rendered_procfs::guest_procfs_root()?;
3689            (uid, gid)
3690        };
3691        let mark = crate::tool_global::directory_snapshot_mark(guest).await;
3692        let ino = self
3693            .directory_inode(guest, binding.device, &binding.within(), mark)
3694            .await;
3695        let epoch = super::rendered_procfs::epoch_time(guest.config().epoch);
3696        Ok(DetStat {
3697            mask: StatxMask::STATX_BASIC_STATS | StatxMask::STATX_MNT_ID,
3698            mode: libc::S_IFDIR | 0o555,
3699            uid,
3700            gid,
3701            attributes: StatxAttributes::empty(),
3702            attributes_mask: StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_AUTOMOUNT
3703                | StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_MOUNT_ROOT
3704                | StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_DAX,
3705            dev: Self::reported_procfs_device(guest),
3706            rdev: 0,
3707            inode: ino,
3708            nlink,
3709            size: 0,
3710            blksize: procfs_inode::PROC_BLOCK_SIZE,
3711            blocks: 0,
3712            atime: epoch,
3713            btime: epoch,
3714            ctime: epoch,
3715            mtime: epoch,
3716        })
3717    }
3718
3719    /// The metadata of the system-wide directory `directory`, as the guest's
3720    /// stat of it reports it where the kernel serves it (ptrace,
3721    /// `Detcore::determinize_stat` of a procfs directory): the kernel's mode,
3722    /// links, owner, size and blocks, as the guest's user namespace sees them
3723    /// (`SystemObject`, from the object the lookup reached: global root unmapped, the
3724    /// network namespace owner's root for `/proc/sys/net`, and on a
3725    /// `proc_dir_entry` directory such as `/proc/tty` two links and one per
3726    /// subdirectory), procfs's 1024-byte blocks and device, the inode number
3727    /// Detcore's pool gives its path, which its `.` entry gets in a listing
3728    /// of it ([`Self::system_placeholder_snapshot`]), and the epoch.
3729    async fn system_directory_metadata<G: Guest<Self>>(
3730        &self,
3731        guest: &mut G,
3732        directory: &SystemPlaceholder,
3733    ) -> Result<DetStat, Error> {
3734        let kernel = directory.object;
3735        let device = Self::procfs_key_device(guest);
3736        let mark = crate::tool_global::directory_snapshot_mark(guest).await;
3737        let ino = self
3738            .directory_inode(guest, device, &directory.within(), mark)
3739            .await;
3740        let epoch = super::rendered_procfs::epoch_time(guest.config().epoch);
3741        Ok(DetStat {
3742            mask: StatxMask::STATX_BASIC_STATS | StatxMask::STATX_MNT_ID,
3743            mode: libc::S_IFDIR | (kernel.mode & 0o7777),
3744            uid: kernel.uid,
3745            gid: kernel.gid,
3746            attributes: StatxAttributes::empty(),
3747            attributes_mask: StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_AUTOMOUNT
3748                | StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_MOUNT_ROOT
3749                | StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_DAX,
3750            dev: Self::reported_procfs_device(guest),
3751            rdev: 0,
3752            inode: ino,
3753            nlink: kernel.nlink,
3754            size: kernel.size,
3755            blksize: procfs_inode::PROC_BLOCK_SIZE,
3756            blocks: kernel.blocks,
3757            atime: epoch,
3758            btime: epoch,
3759            ctime: epoch,
3760            mtime: epoch,
3761        })
3762    }
3763
3764    /// The metadata of the caller's `/proc/self` or `/proc/thread-self` link
3765    /// itself (`spelling`, not followed), as Linux reports it
3766    /// (`proc_setup_self`, `proc_setup_thread_self`, fs/proc/self.c and
3767    /// fs/proc/thread_self.c at v6.17): a symbolic link of mode 0777, one
3768    /// link, global root's, as the guest's own stat of `/proc` shows that
3769    /// owner (`guest_procfs_root`), size 0, procfs's device and blocks, the
3770    /// inode number its path keys, and the epoch.
3771    async fn selector_link_metadata<G: Guest<Self>>(
3772        &self,
3773        guest: &mut G,
3774        spelling: &Path,
3775    ) -> Result<DetStat, Error> {
3776        let (_, uid, gid) = super::rendered_procfs::guest_procfs_root()?;
3777        let device = Self::procfs_key_device(guest);
3778        let within = Path::new("/").join(spelling.file_name().unwrap_or_default());
3779        let mark = crate::tool_global::directory_snapshot_mark(guest).await;
3780        let inodes = DirectoryInodes::new(device, Some(ProcfsDirectory::new(PathBuf::from("/"))));
3781        let name = within.file_name().unwrap_or_default().as_bytes().to_vec();
3782        let (host, procfs_name) =
3783            inodes.entry_inode(&name, procfs_inode::path_inode(device, &within).ino);
3784        let ino = crate::tool_global::determinize_listed_inode(guest, host, procfs_name, mark)
3785            .await
3786            .0
3787            .as_raw();
3788        let epoch = super::rendered_procfs::epoch_time(guest.config().epoch);
3789        Ok(DetStat {
3790            mask: StatxMask::STATX_BASIC_STATS | StatxMask::STATX_MNT_ID,
3791            mode: libc::S_IFLNK | 0o777,
3792            uid,
3793            gid,
3794            attributes: StatxAttributes::empty(),
3795            attributes_mask: StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_AUTOMOUNT
3796                | StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_MOUNT_ROOT
3797                | StatxAttributes::STATX_ATTR_DAX,
3798            dev: Self::reported_procfs_device(guest),
3799            rdev: 0,
3800            inode: ino,
3801            nlink: 1,
3802            size: 0,
3803            blksize: procfs_inode::PROC_BLOCK_SIZE,
3804            blocks: 0,
3805            atime: epoch,
3806            btime: epoch,
3807            ctime: epoch,
3808            mtime: epoch,
3809        })
3810    }
3811
3812    /// The fdinfo Linux gives placeholder descriptor `fd`, as the kernel's
3813    /// fdinfo for it would need to be (it describes the placeholder: its tag
3814    /// as the position, its tmpfs mount and inode): Detcore's position, the
3815    /// guest's procfs mount and inode 0, which the snapshot's sanitizer
3816    /// replaces with the directory's inode number (`procfs::sanitize_fdinfo`),
3817    /// returned beside it, as the placeholder's `fstat` numbers it. `None`
3818    /// for any other descriptor.
3819    pub(super) async fn placeholder_fdinfo<G: Guest<Self>>(
3820        &self,
3821        guest: &mut G,
3822        fd: RawFd,
3823    ) -> Result<Option<(Vec<u8>, u64)>, Error> {
3824        let Some(state) = Self::placeholder_of(guest, fd) else {
3825            return Ok(None);
3826        };
3827        let position = match state.directory {
3828            PlaceholderDirectory::Root
3829            | PlaceholderDirectory::Task(_)
3830            | PlaceholderDirectory::System(_) => guest
3831                .thread_state()
3832                .with_detfd(fd, |detfd| detfd.directory_stream_position())?,
3833            PlaceholderDirectory::Descriptors(_) => state.position,
3834        };
3835        let contents = super::rendered_procfs::rendered_target_fdinfo(
3836            position,
3837            super::rendered_procfs::guest_procfs_mount_id()?,
3838        );
3839        let inode = self
3840            .placeholder_metadata(guest, &state.directory, Some(fd))
3841            .await?
3842            .inode;
3843        Ok(Some((contents, inode)))
3844    }
3845
3846    /// The metadata of the placeholder directory a stat names, if Detcore
3847    /// serves it: a descriptor that is one, a path that spells `/proc` or one
3848    /// of the caller's own descriptor directories, or, followed, a descriptor
3849    /// link of the caller's to a placeholder (`/proc/self/fd/D`; not
3850    /// followed, that is the link, which the kernel describes). A spelling of
3851    /// `/proc` through a task that does not exist is `ENOENT`, where Linux's
3852    /// walk stops ([`NamedTask`]).
3853    pub(super) async fn placeholder_stat<G: Guest<Self>>(
3854        &self,
3855        guest: &mut G,
3856        target: StatTarget<'_>,
3857    ) -> Result<Option<DetStat>, Error> {
3858        if !Self::serves_placeholders(guest) {
3859            return Ok(None);
3860        }
3861        let (directory, object) = match target {
3862            StatTarget::Descriptor(fd) => match Self::placeholder_of(guest, fd) {
3863                Some(state) => (state.directory, Some(fd)),
3864                None => return Ok(None),
3865            },
3866            StatTarget::Path {
3867                dirfd,
3868                path: Some(pointer),
3869                follow,
3870            } => {
3871                let path = pointer.read(&guest.memory())?;
3872                if path.as_os_str().is_empty()
3873                    || Self::starts_at_a_recorded_link(guest, dirfd, &path)
3874                {
3875                    return Ok(None);
3876                }
3877                let Some((pid, tid)) = Self::descriptor_directory_caller(guest) else {
3878                    return Ok(None);
3879                };
3880                let spelling = self.procfs_spelling_at(guest, dirfd, &path).await?;
3881                let (spelling, links) = Self::decided_path(guest, &spelling)?;
3882                // The caller's `/proc/self` or `/proc/thread-self` link
3883                // itself, by any spelling that ends at it (`..` resolved:
3884                // `/proc/self/../self`), where Detcore serves the directories
3885                // it reaches, after the walk to it; a final `/` or `/.` of
3886                // the guest's spelling follows it to the directory, as Linux
3887                // follows a trailing slash (`LOOKUP_FOLLOW`).
3888                if !follow
3889                    && super::opens_the_link_itself(&path, OFlag::O_NOFOLLOW)
3890                    && let Some(link) = Self::own_selector_link(guest, &spelling, links).await
3891                {
3892                    let link = link?;
3893                    return Ok(Some(self.selector_link_metadata(guest, &link).await?));
3894                }
3895                if let Some(directory) = Self::own_task_directory(guest, &spelling, links).await {
3896                    let (kind, process, thread) = directory?;
3897                    let binding = self
3898                        .task_directory_binding(guest, kind, (process, thread))
3899                        .await;
3900                    return Ok(Some(
3901                        self.placeholder_metadata(
3902                            guest,
3903                            &PlaceholderDirectory::Task(binding),
3904                            None,
3905                        )
3906                        .await?,
3907                    ));
3908                }
3909                let directory = match fd_directory::classify(&spelling, (pid, tid)) {
3910                    Some(table) => {
3911                        let thread = (table == DescriptorDirectoryTable::Thread).then_some(tid);
3912                        let answer = crate::tool_global::procfs_task(
3913                            guest,
3914                            crate::procfs::BoundTask {
3915                                process: pid,
3916                                thread,
3917                            },
3918                        )
3919                        .await;
3920                        PlaceholderDirectory::Descriptors(DescriptorDirectoryBinding {
3921                            table,
3922                            pid,
3923                            tid,
3924                            process_incarnation: answer.process_incarnation,
3925                            thread_incarnation: answer.thread_incarnation,
3926                            device: Self::procfs_key_device(guest),
3927                            files: (table == DescriptorDirectoryTable::Thread)
3928                                .then(|| guest.thread_state().files_id()),
3929                        })
3930                    }
3931                    None => match names_procfs_root(&spelling) {
3932                        Some(tasks) if Self::named_tasks_exist(guest, &tasks).await => {
3933                            PlaceholderDirectory::Root
3934                        }
3935                        Some(_) => return Err(Errno::ENOENT.into()),
3936                        None if follow => match Self::own_link_directory(guest, &spelling) {
3937                            // Following this last link too, within the one
3938                            // budget.
3939                            Some(_) if links + selector_links(&spelling) + 1 > LINK_LIMIT => {
3940                                return Err(Errno::ELOOP.into());
3941                            }
3942                            Some(directory) => directory,
3943                            None => match self
3944                                .system_directory_reached(guest, &path, &spelling, links, true)?
3945                            {
3946                                Some(directory) => directory,
3947                                None => return Ok(None),
3948                            },
3949                        },
3950                        // A final `/` or `/.` follows a final link, as for
3951                        // the selector links above.
3952                        None => match self.system_directory_reached(
3953                            guest,
3954                            &path,
3955                            &spelling,
3956                            links,
3957                            !super::opens_the_link_itself(&path, OFlag::O_NOFOLLOW),
3958                        )? {
3959                            Some(directory) => directory,
3960                            None => return Ok(None),
3961                        },
3962                    },
3963                };
3964                (directory, None)
3965            }
3966            StatTarget::Path { path: None, .. } | StatTarget::Cwd | StatTarget::Overwritten => {
3967                return Ok(None);
3968            }
3969        };
3970        Ok(Some(
3971            self.placeholder_metadata(guest, &directory, object).await?,
3972        ))
3973    }
3974
3975    /// The named refusal of `sysno` for `reason` (ARCHITECTURE.md rule 6),
3976    /// logged as Detcore's other refusals are, then stopping the run, or,
3977    /// with `--allow-unsupported-syscalls`, giving the guest `fallback`
3978    /// (`Detcore::refuse_unserviceable_operation_because`).
3979    async fn refuse_placeholder_call<G: Guest<Self>>(
3980        &self,
3981        guest: &mut G,
3982        sysno: Sysno,
3983        fallback: Errno,
3984        reason: &str,
3985    ) -> Result<i64, Error> {
3986        tracing::error!("Detcore refuses {sysno}: {reason}");
3987        self.refuse_unserviceable_operation_because(guest, sysno, fallback, reason)
3988            .await
3989    }
3990
3991    /// Answer `call` if it operates on a placeholder descriptor
3992    /// ([`placeholder_operand`]), from Detcore's model, with one implementation
3993    /// whatever the descriptor's object ([`PlaceholderObject`]), so that no
3994    /// call through it reaches that object's filesystem, whose answers are
3995    /// not the directory's. The calls, and their answers, as Linux answers
3996    /// them for procfs's root and for a descriptor directory, each in Linux's
3997    /// order of checks (v6.17; measured on Linux 7.1):
3998    /// - `getdents`, `getdents64`: the listing (above); `lseek`:
3999    ///   `generic_file_llseek` on an empty directory (`fd_directory::seek`),
4000    ///   on the listing's position;
4001    /// - `fchdir`: the directory entered ([`Self::enter_placeholder`]);
4002    /// - `fcntl` ([`Self::placeholder_fcntl`]): the status flags, as `setfl`
4003    ///   keeps them; the table commands, which reach the descriptor; the
4004    ///   query commands; the write hints, kept for the directory's inode; the
4005    ///   record locks refused by name (`ENOLCK`: named residual); the lease,
4006    ///   seal, pipe and notify commands as Linux answers them for a directory;
4007    /// - `read` and its relatives: the position's `EINVAL` where one is given,
4008    ///   the buffer's `access_ok` (`EFAULT`), the vectors, then `EISDIR`
4009    ///   ([`read_answer`]); `write` and its relatives: a negative position's
4010    ///   `EINVAL`, then `EBADF`, the description cannot write;
4011    /// - `ioctl` ([`Self::placeholder_ioctl`]);
4012    /// - `fsync`, `fdatasync`: `EINVAL` (procfs has no `fsync`); `syncfs`: 0;
4013    /// - `flock`: each directory its own lock (`procfs::locks::LockLedger::
4014    ///   placeholder_flock`), as each is its own inode on Linux;
4015    /// - `fchmod` and the path-less `fchmodat2`: `EPERM`; `fchown` and the
4016    ///   path-less `fchownat`: 0, Detcore's virtual root's answer
4017    ///   (`handle_ownership_change_noop`) with no change to report;
4018    /// - the `f*xattr` calls: procfs's answers (no attribute handlers);
4019    /// - `ftruncate`: a negative length's `EINVAL`, then `EINVAL` (not a
4020    ///   regular file); `fallocate`: `vfs_fallocate`'s checks, then `EBADF`;
4021    ///   `fadvise64`: `generic_fadvise` on a filesystem with no backing
4022    ///   device; `readahead`: `EINVAL`; `sync_file_range`: its argument
4023    ///   checks, then 0;
4024    /// - `fstatfs`: procfs's (`write_procfs_statfs`);
4025    /// - a file-backed `mmap`: as for a procfs file without `mmap`
4026    ///   (`answer_rendered_procfs_mapping`);
4027    /// - the path-less `utimensat`: `EPERM` with times or `EACCES` without on
4028    ///   global root's `/proc`, 0 on the caller's own directory;
4029    ///   `faccessat2`: `EACCES` for write access to `/proc`, else 0;
4030    /// - `sendfile`, `splice`, `tee` and `copy_file_range` with a placeholder
4031    ///   on either side ([`Self::answer_placeholder_transfer`]).
4032    ///
4033    /// An `O_PATH` descriptor fails with `EBADF` where Linux's descriptor
4034    /// lookup refuses it (`fdget`), which is first for most calls and after
4035    /// the arguments a call checks first for the others (a negative position
4036    /// or length, `flock`'s command, the attribute name); `fchdir`, `fstatfs`,
4037    /// `fcntl`'s table and query commands and the path calls take it. A
4038    /// placeholder Detcore keeps no record of (one received without
4039    /// sequentialized threads) has no state to answer from, and its call is
4040    /// refused by name. `None` for every other call.
4041    pub(crate) async fn answer_placeholder_call<G: Guest<Self>>(
4042        &self,
4043        guest: &mut G,
4044        call: &Syscall,
4045    ) -> Option<Result<i64, Error>> {
4046        let (sysno, args) = (*call).into_parts();
4047        if !Self::serves_placeholders(guest) {
4048            return None;
4049        }
4050        // Calls with two descriptor operands, either a placeholder.
4051        if matches!(
4052            sysno,
4053            Sysno::sendfile | Sysno::splice | Sysno::tee | Sysno::copy_file_range
4054        ) {
4055            return self.answer_placeholder_transfer(guest, sysno, &args).await;
4056        }
4057        let fd_position = placeholder_operand(&guest.memory(), sysno, &args)?;
4058        let fd = argument(&args, fd_position) as i32;
4059        let state = match Self::placeholder_of(guest, fd) {
4060            Some(state) => state,
4061            None => {
4062                let directory = Self::unrecorded_placeholder(guest, fd).await?;
4063                return Some(
4064                    self.refuse_placeholder_call(
4065                        guest,
4066                        sysno,
4067                        Errno::EBADF,
4068                        &format!(
4069                            "descriptor {fd} stands for {}, a /proc directory Detcore serves, \
4070                             but was received without sequentialized threads, and Detcore \
4071                             keeps no state to answer it from",
4072                            directory.canonical().display()
4073                        ),
4074                    )
4075                    .await,
4076                );
4077            }
4078        };
4079        // `flock` ignores `LOCK_MAND` before it looks at the descriptor.
4080        if sysno == Sysno::flock && args.arg1 as i32 & LOCK_MAND != 0 {
4081            return Some(Ok(0));
4082        }
4083        // The calls whose descriptor lookup (`fdget`, `fdget_pos`) refuses
4084        // an `O_PATH` descriptor before they look at anything else.
4085        // x86_64 only: aarch64 has getdents64 alone.
4086        #[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
4087        let x86_only_path_only = matches!(sysno, Sysno::getdents | Sysno::sync_file_range);
4088        #[cfg(not(target_arch = "x86_64"))]
4089        let x86_only_path_only = false;
4090        #[cfg(target_arch = "aarch64")]
4091        let aarch64_path_only = sysno == Sysno::sync_file_range;
4092        #[cfg(not(target_arch = "aarch64"))]
4093        let aarch64_path_only = false;
4094        let path_only_refused = state.path_only
4095            && (matches!(
4096                sysno,
4097                Sysno::getdents64
4098                    | Sysno::lseek
4099                    | Sysno::read
4100                    | Sysno::readv
4101                    | Sysno::write
4102                    | Sysno::writev
4103                    | Sysno::ioctl
4104                    | Sysno::fsync
4105                    | Sysno::fdatasync
4106                    | Sysno::syncfs
4107                    | Sysno::fchmod
4108                    | Sysno::fchown
4109                    | Sysno::fallocate
4110                    | Sysno::fadvise64
4111                    | Sysno::readahead
4112                    | Sysno::flistxattr
4113                    | Sysno::mmap
4114            )
4115            || x86_only_path_only
4116            // aarch64 number 84: sys_sync_file_range semantics (above).
4117            || aarch64_path_only);
4118        if path_only_refused {
4119            return Some(Err(Errno::EBADF.into()));
4120        }
4121        // The calls that check an argument before their descriptor lookup
4122        // refuses an `O_PATH` descriptor: a negative position or length.
4123        let positioned = |position: i64, minus_one_reads_on: bool| {
4124            if position < 0 && !(minus_one_reads_on && position == -1) {
4125                Some(Err(Errno::EINVAL.into()))
4126            } else if state.path_only {
4127                Some(Err(Errno::EBADF.into()))
4128            } else {
4129                None
4130            }
4131        };
4132        let early = match sysno {
4133            Sysno::pread64 | Sysno::pwrite64 | Sysno::preadv | Sysno::pwritev => {
4134                positioned(args.arg3 as i64, false)
4135            }
4136            Sysno::preadv2 | Sysno::pwritev2 => positioned(args.arg3 as i64, true),
4137            Sysno::ftruncate => positioned(args.arg1 as i64, false),
4138            _ => None,
4139        };
4140        if let Some(answer) = early {
4141            return Some(answer);
4142        }
4143        Some(match call {
4144            Syscall::Getdents64(_)
4145                if matches!(
4146                    state.directory,
4147                    PlaceholderDirectory::Root
4148                        | PlaceholderDirectory::Task(_)
4149                        | PlaceholderDirectory::System(_)
4150                ) =>
4151            {
4152                self.serve_root_placeholder(guest, call, fd, state).await
4153            }
4154            // x86_64 only: aarch64 has Getdents64 alone.
4155            #[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
4156            Syscall::Getdents(_)
4157                if matches!(
4158                    state.directory,
4159                    PlaceholderDirectory::Root
4160                        | PlaceholderDirectory::Task(_)
4161                        | PlaceholderDirectory::System(_)
4162                ) =>
4163            {
4164                self.serve_root_placeholder(guest, call, fd, state).await
4165            }
4166            #[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
4167            Syscall::Getdents(c) => {
4168                self.serve_descriptor_directory(
4169                    guest,
4170                    fd,
4171                    state,
4172                    c.dirent().map(|dirent| dirent.cast::<u8>()),
4173                    c.count() as usize,
4174                    DirentFormat::Legacy,
4175                )
4176                .await
4177            }
4178            Syscall::Getdents64(c) => {
4179                self.serve_descriptor_directory(
4180                    guest,
4181                    fd,
4182                    state,
4183                    c.dirent().map(|dirent| dirent.cast::<u8>()),
4184                    c.count() as usize,
4185                    DirentFormat::Dirent64,
4186                )
4187                .await
4188            }
4189            Syscall::Lseek(c) => {
4190                self.seek_placeholder(guest, fd, state, c.offset(), args.arg2 as i32)
4191            }
4192            Syscall::Fchdir(_) => self.enter_placeholder(guest, fd, &state.directory).await?,
4193            Syscall::Fcntl(c) => self.placeholder_fcntl(guest, fd, state, c).await?,
4194            Syscall::Read(_)
4195            | Syscall::Readv(_)
4196            | Syscall::Pread64(_)
4197            | Syscall::Preadv(_)
4198            | Syscall::Preadv2(_) => read_answer(&guest.memory(), sysno, &args).map_err(Into::into),
4199            Syscall::Write(_)
4200            | Syscall::Writev(_)
4201            | Syscall::Pwrite64(_)
4202            | Syscall::Pwritev(_)
4203            | Syscall::Pwritev2(_) => Err(Errno::EBADF.into()),
4204            Syscall::Ioctl(_) => self
4205                .placeholder_ioctl(guest, fd, args.arg1 as u32, args.arg2)
4206                .await?
4207                .map_err(Into::into),
4208            Syscall::Fsync(_) | Syscall::Fdatasync(_) => Err(Errno::EINVAL.into()),
4209            Syscall::Flock(c) => {
4210                self.placeholder_flock(guest, fd, state, c.operation())
4211                    .await
4212            }
4213            Syscall::Fchmod(_) => Err(Errno::EPERM.into()),
4214            Syscall::Fchown(_) => Ok(0),
4215            Syscall::Fgetxattr(_)
4216            | Syscall::Fsetxattr(_)
4217            | Syscall::Flistxattr(_)
4218            | Syscall::Fremovexattr(_) => self.placeholder_xattr(guest, call, state).await,
4219            Syscall::Ftruncate(_) => Err(Errno::EINVAL.into()),
4220            Syscall::Fallocate(_) => {
4221                Err(fallocate_answer(args.arg1 as i32, args.arg2 as i64, args.arg3 as i64).into())
4222            }
4223            Syscall::Readahead(_) => Err(Errno::EINVAL.into()),
4224            Syscall::Fstatfs(c) => self.write_procfs_statfs(guest, c.buf()).map(|()| 0),
4225            Syscall::Mmap(_) => return None,
4226            _ => match sysno {
4227                Sysno::syncfs => Ok(0),
4228                Sysno::fadvise64 => {
4229                    fadvise_answer(args.arg2 as i64, args.arg3 as i32).map_err(Into::into)
4230                }
4231                #[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
4232                Sysno::sync_file_range => {
4233                    sync_file_range_answer(args.arg1 as i64, args.arg2 as i64, args.arg3 as u32)
4234                        .map_err(Into::into)
4235                }
4236                // aarch64 number 84 is sys_sync_file_range with the
4237                // same (fd, offset, nbytes, flags) order (see above).
4238                #[cfg(target_arch = "aarch64")]
4239                Sysno::sync_file_range => {
4240                    sync_file_range_answer(args.arg1 as i64, args.arg2 as i64, args.arg3 as u32)
4241                        .map_err(Into::into)
4242                }
4243                Sysno::utimensat => {
4244                    self.placeholder_utimensat(
4245                        guest,
4246                        state,
4247                        args.arg1 == 0,
4248                        args.arg2,
4249                        args.arg3 as i32,
4250                    )
4251                    .await
4252                }
4253                // `do_fchmodat`'s flags, then procfs's answer to a mode change.
4254                Sysno::fchmodat2
4255                    if args.arg3 as i32 & !(libc::AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW | libc::AT_EMPTY_PATH)
4256                        != 0 =>
4257                {
4258                    Err(Errno::EINVAL.into())
4259                }
4260                Sysno::fchmodat2 => Err(Errno::EPERM.into()),
4261                // `do_fchownat`'s flags, then Detcore's virtual root's answer.
4262                Sysno::fchownat
4263                    if args.arg4 as i32 & !(libc::AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW | libc::AT_EMPTY_PATH)
4264                        != 0 =>
4265                {
4266                    Err(Errno::EINVAL.into())
4267                }
4268                Sysno::fchownat => Ok(0),
4269                Sysno::faccessat2 => self
4270                    .placeholder_access(&state.directory, args.arg2 as i32, args.arg3 as i32)
4271                    .map_err(Into::into),
4272                _ => return None,
4273            },
4274        })
4275    }
4276
4277    /// Whether `call` is one [`Self::refuse_lookup_below_placeholder`] may
4278    /// refuse, by its number alone, so that every other call skips it.
4279    pub(crate) fn looks_up_below_placeholders(call: &Syscall) -> bool {
4280        matches!(
4281            call.number(),
4282            Sysno::execve
4283                | Sysno::execveat
4284                | Sysno::bind
4285                | Sysno::connect
4286                | Sysno::sendto
4287                | Sysno::sendmsg
4288                | Sysno::sendmmsg
4289        ) && OPENED_ANY.load(Ordering::SeqCst)
4290    }
4291
4292    /// The named refusal (ARCHITECTURE.md rule 6) of a call whose path leads
4293    /// below a placeholder directory, and which Detcore does not respell for
4294    /// the backend ([`Self::respelled_lookup`]): `execve` and `execveat`,
4295    /// which read their image before an injection could carry a respelled
4296    /// path, and the pathname `AF_UNIX` addresses of `bind`, `connect`,
4297    /// `sendto`, `sendmsg` and `sendmmsg`, which live in the guest's own
4298    /// structures. Such a path would reach the placeholder's object, not the
4299    /// directory, where Linux reaches the directory: on ptrace only a
4300    /// descriptor directory (`/proc` itself is its descriptor's object), on
4301    /// KVM either. A path that is a descriptor link and nothing more is left
4302    /// to the backend: an exec of a directory and an `AF_UNIX` address on one
4303    /// fail alike there (`EACCES`, `ECONNREFUSED` or `EADDRINUSE`). A send's
4304    /// address is looked up only by a datagram socket (`unix_dgram_sendmsg`):
4305    /// a stream socket refuses it without a lookup (`EISCONN` or
4306    /// `EOPNOTSUPP`, `unix_stream_sendmsg`) and a sequenced-packet socket
4307    /// ignores it (`unix_seqpacket_sendmsg`), so their sends go on as Linux's
4308    /// do; where the socket's type cannot be asked, the send is refused.
4309    /// `None` for every other call and path.
4310    pub(crate) async fn refuse_lookup_below_placeholder<G: Guest<Self>>(
4311        &self,
4312        guest: &mut G,
4313        call: &Syscall,
4314    ) -> Option<Result<i64, Error>> {
4315        if !OPENED_ANY.load(Ordering::SeqCst) || !Self::serves_placeholders(guest) {
4316            return None;
4317        }
4318        let (sysno, args) = call.into_parts();
4319        let read_path = |guest: &G, pointer: usize| -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
4320            PathPtr::from_ptr(pointer as *const libc::c_char)
4321                .and_then(|pointer| pointer.read(&guest.memory()).ok())
4322                .map(|path: PathBuf| path.into_os_string().into_encoded_bytes())
4323                .filter(|path| !path.is_empty())
4324        };
4325        let lookups: Vec<(Option<RawFd>, Vec<u8>)> = match sysno {
4326            Sysno::execve => read_path(guest, args.arg0)
4327                .map(|path| (None, path))
4328                .into_iter()
4329                .collect(),
4330            Sysno::execveat => read_path(guest, args.arg1)
4331                .map(|path| (Some(args.arg0 as RawFd), path))
4332                .into_iter()
4333                .collect(),
4334            Sysno::bind | Sysno::connect => {
4335                unix_address_path(&guest.memory(), args.arg1, args.arg2)
4336                    .map(|path| (None, path))
4337                    .into_iter()
4338                    .collect()
4339            }
4340            Sysno::sendto => unix_address_path(&guest.memory(), args.arg4, args.arg5)
4341                .map(|path| (None, path))
4342                .into_iter()
4343                .collect(),
4344            Sysno::sendmsg => message_addresses(&guest.memory(), args.arg1, 1, 0)
4345                .into_iter()
4346                .map(|path| (None, path))
4347                .collect(),
4348            Sysno::sendmmsg => message_addresses(&guest.memory(), args.arg1, args.arg2, 64)
4349                .into_iter()
4350                .map(|path| (None, path))
4351                .collect(),
4352            _ => return None,
4353        };
4354        let send = matches!(sysno, Sysno::sendto | Sysno::sendmsg | Sysno::sendmmsg);
4355        for (dirfd, path) in lookups {
4356            let looked_up = Self::respelled_lookup(guest, dirfd, &path, false, 0).await;
4357            if matches!(looked_up, Ok(None)) {
4358                continue;
4359            }
4360            // Only a datagram socket looks a send's address up, so no answer
4361            // of the lookup's, its errors included, is a stream or
4362            // sequenced-packet socket's; one socket makes every send of the
4363            // call.
4364            if send
4365                && self
4366                    .socket_is_datagram(guest, args.arg0 as RawFd)
4367                    .await
4368                    .is_some_and(|datagram| !datagram)
4369            {
4370                return None;
4371            }
4372            if let Err(errno) = looked_up {
4373                return Some(Err(errno.into()));
4374            }
4375            return Some(
4376                self.refuse_placeholder_call(
4377                    guest,
4378                    sysno,
4379                    Errno::ENOENT,
4380                    &format!(
4381                        "the path {} leads below a /proc directory Detcore serves on a \
4382                         placeholder descriptor, and Detcore cannot spell it for this call as \
4383                         Linux resolves it",
4384                        String::from_utf8_lossy(&path)
4385                    ),
4386                )
4387                .await,
4388            );
4389        }
4390        None
4391    }
4392
4393    /// `lseek` of a placeholder descriptor, which is `generic_file_llseek` on
4394    /// an empty directory ([`fd_directory::seek`]) on both: a descriptor
4395    /// directory's position, and `/proc`'s, the position of its directory
4396    /// stream, whose snapshot a seek to 0 drops, as `rewinddir` refreshes
4397    /// one (`DirectoryStream::seek`).
4398    fn seek_placeholder<G: Guest<Self>>(
4399        &self,
4400        guest: &mut G,
4401        fd: RawFd,
4402        state: PlaceholderState,
4403        offset: i64,
4404        whence: i32,
4405    ) -> Result<i64, Error> {
4406        match state.directory {
4407            PlaceholderDirectory::Descriptors(_) => {
4408                let position = fd_directory::seek(state.position, offset, whence)?;
4409                guest.thread_state().with_detfd(fd, |detfd| {
4410                    detfd.set_placeholder(PlaceholderState { position, ..state })
4411                })?;
4412                Ok(position as i64)
4413            }
4414            PlaceholderDirectory::Root
4415            | PlaceholderDirectory::Task(_)
4416            | PlaceholderDirectory::System(_) => {
4417                let current = guest
4418                    .thread_state()
4419                    .with_detfd(fd, |detfd| detfd.directory_stream_position())?;
4420                let position = fd_directory::seek(current, offset, whence)?;
4421                guest
4422                    .thread_state()
4423                    .with_detfd(fd, |detfd| detfd.seek_directory_stream(position))?;
4424                Ok(position as i64)
4425            }
4426        }
4427    }
4428
4429    /// `fchdir` to placeholder descriptor `fd`, which enters the directory it
4430    /// stands for as Linux's `fchdir` enters it (`set_fs_pwd`), so that the
4431    /// working directory, `getcwd`, `/proc/self/cwd` and every name looked
4432    /// up from it are the directory's own:
4433    /// - on procfs's root (ptrace): `/proc` itself is the descriptor's own
4434    ///   object, which the guest's `fchdir` enters (`None`: the kernel answers
4435    ///   it); a descriptor directory is entered from the descriptor, in every
4436    ///   state of the caller: one injected `openat` of the directory's path
4437    ///   within procfs (`<pid>/fd`, `O_PATH`), `fchdir` to it and its
4438    ///   `close`. Where the caller's table has no free slot for that
4439    ///   descriptor, one injected `chdir` to the directory's absolute path,
4440    ///   where Detcore knows that path reaches it (the caller has not changed
4441    ///   its root and is in no nested PID namespace), and otherwise a refusal
4442    ///   by name: either way the working directory changes only if the call
4443    ///   succeeds, as Linux's `fchdir` changes it;
4444    /// - on the placeholder (KVM): an injected `chdir` to the directory's
4445    ///   path, which the backend resolves (KVM's executor refuses a working
4446    ///   directory on procfs with `EACCES`, as it does for the guest's own
4447    ///   `chdir("/proc")`).
4448    ///
4449    /// A descriptor directory whose task is gone (or whose numbers another
4450    /// task holds now) is one Linux still enters, a directory below which no
4451    /// lookup finds anything, and whose `getcwd` fails; no object of the
4452    /// backend's stands for it, so that `fchdir` is refused by name.
4453    async fn enter_placeholder<G: Guest<Self>>(
4454        &self,
4455        guest: &mut G,
4456        fd: RawFd,
4457        directory: &PlaceholderDirectory,
4458    ) -> Option<Result<i64, Error>> {
4459        let object = Self::placeholder_object(guest);
4460        if object == PlaceholderObject::ProcfsRoot && *directory == PlaceholderDirectory::Root {
4461            return None;
4462        }
4463        if !Self::placeholder_current(guest, directory).await {
4464            return Some(
4465                self.refuse_placeholder_call(
4466                    guest,
4467                    Sysno::fchdir,
4468                    Errno::ENOENT,
4469                    &format!(
4470                        "fchdir to {}, a descriptor directory whose task has exited, which \
4471                         Linux enters though nothing below it exists; Detcore has no directory \
4472                         to stand for it",
4473                        directory.canonical().display()
4474                    ),
4475                )
4476                .await,
4477            );
4478        }
4479        Some(match (object, directory) {
4480            (PlaceholderObject::ProcfsRoot, PlaceholderDirectory::Descriptors(binding)) => {
4481                let within = binding.within().into_os_string().into_encoded_bytes();
4482                let within = &within[1..];
4483                match Self::inject_openat(
4484                    guest,
4485                    fd,
4486                    within,
4487                    OFlag::O_PATH | OFlag::O_DIRECTORY | OFlag::O_CLOEXEC,
4488                )
4489                .await
4490                {
4491                    Ok(opened) => {
4492                        let entered = guest
4493                            .inject_with_retry(syscalls::Fchdir::new().with_fd(opened))
4494                            .await;
4495                        let _ = guest.inject(syscalls::Close::new().with_fd(opened)).await;
4496                        entered.map_err(Into::into)
4497                    }
4498                    Err(Errno::EMFILE | Errno::ENFILE)
4499                        if Self::descriptor_directory_caller(guest).is_some() =>
4500                    {
4501                        let canonical = binding.canonical();
4502                        Self::inject_chdir(guest, canonical.as_os_str().as_bytes())
4503                            .await
4504                            .map_err(Into::into)
4505                    }
4506                    Err(Errno::EMFILE | Errno::ENFILE) => {
4507                        self.refuse_placeholder_call(
4508                            guest,
4509                            Sysno::fchdir,
4510                            Errno::EMFILE,
4511                            &format!(
4512                                "fchdir to {}, which Detcore enters through one more \
4513                                 descriptor, while the caller's table has no free slot for it \
4514                                 and its root or PID namespace keeps the directory's absolute \
4515                                 path from naming it",
4516                                binding.canonical().display()
4517                            ),
4518                        )
4519                        .await
4520                    }
4521                    Err(errno) => Err(errno.into()),
4522                }
4523            }
4524            _ => {
4525                let canonical = directory.canonical();
4526                Self::inject_chdir(guest, canonical.as_os_str().as_bytes())
4527                    .await
4528                    .map_err(Into::into)
4529            }
4530        })
4531    }
4532
4533    /// One injected `chdir(path)`, with the path staged on Reverie's guest
4534    /// stack.
4535    async fn inject_chdir<G: Guest<Self>>(guest: &mut G, path: &[u8]) -> Result<i64, Errno> {
4536        const PATH_CAPACITY: usize = 64;
4537        if path.len() >= PATH_CAPACITY {
4538            return Err(Errno::ENAMETOOLONG);
4539        }
4540        let mut stack = guest.stack().await;
4541        let staged = stack.push(super::rendered_procfs::c_string::<PATH_CAPACITY>(path));
4542        let stack_guard = stack.commit().map_err(|_| Errno::EFAULT)?;
4543        let entered = guest
4544            .inject_with_retry(
4545                syscalls::Chdir::new()
4546                    .with_path(PathPtr::from_ptr(staged.as_raw() as *const libc::c_char)),
4547            )
4548            .await;
4549        drop(stack_guard);
4550        entered
4551    }
4552
4553    /// `fcntl` through a placeholder descriptor (see
4554    /// [`Self::answer_placeholder_call`]), as `do_fcntl` answers it for the
4555    /// directory, or `None` for a command that reaches the descriptor
4556    /// (v6.17 `fs/fcntl.c`):
4557    /// - an `O_PATH` descriptor takes only `F_DUPFD`, `F_DUPFD_CLOEXEC`,
4558    ///   `F_DUPFD_QUERY`, `F_GETFD`, `F_SETFD`, `F_GETFL` and
4559    ///   `F_CREATED_QUERY` (`check_fcntl_cmd`), every other `EBADF`;
4560    /// - `F_GETFL`: the description's status flags; `F_SETFL` (`setfl`):
4561    ///   setting `O_NOATIME` needs the directory's owner or one who may act
4562    ///   as it (`EPERM` on global root's `/proc`), `O_DIRECT` is `EINVAL` (no
4563    ///   direct I/O), and only `SETFL_MASK` is kept, which `O_ASYNC` is not
4564    ///   (a directory has no `fasync`: the call succeeds, the flag stays as
4565    ///   the open left it);
4566    /// - `F_DUPFD_QUERY`: whether another descriptor names the same
4567    ///   description (`EBADF` for one not open); `F_CREATED_QUERY`: 0, the
4568    ///   open created no directory;
4569    /// - the write hints are the directory's inode's (`i_write_hint`), kept
4570    ///   for every open of it (`procfs::locks::LockLedger::
4571    ///   placeholder_write_hint`): `F_GET_RW_HINT` writes it, 0 until set;
4572    ///   `F_SET_RW_HINT` needs the owner (`EPERM`) before it reads the hint
4573    ///   (`EFAULT`) and checks it (`EINVAL` past 5);
4574    /// - the record locks are refused by name (`ENOLCK`: named residual); a
4575    ///   lease is an invalid type's `EINVAL`, then `EACCES` without the
4576    ///   owner (global root's `/proc`), then `EINVAL` for a directory;
4577    ///   `F_GETLEASE` is `F_UNLCK`; `F_ADD_SEALS` `EPERM` (not writable),
4578    ///   `F_GET_SEALS` `EINVAL`; the pipe sizes `EBADF`; `F_NOTIFY` 0, with no
4579    ///   event ever sent (named residual); any other command `EINVAL`.
4580    async fn placeholder_fcntl<G: Guest<Self>>(
4581        &self,
4582        guest: &mut G,
4583        fd: RawFd,
4584        state: PlaceholderState,
4585        call: &syscalls::Fcntl,
4586    ) -> Option<Result<i64, Error>> {
4587        let (_, args) = Syscall::Fcntl(*call).into_parts();
4588        let command = args.arg1 as libc::c_int;
4589        Some(match command {
4590            libc::F_GETFL => {
4591                let status = guest
4592                    .thread_state()
4593                    .with_detfd(fd, |detfd| detfd.status_flags())
4594                    .unwrap_or(libc::O_DIRECTORY);
4595                Ok(i64::from(status & !libc::O_CLOEXEC))
4596            }
4597            // The table commands, which the kernel's descriptor table answers
4598            // for every file, `O_PATH` ones included.
4599            libc::F_DUPFD | libc::F_DUPFD_CLOEXEC | libc::F_GETFD | libc::F_SETFD => return None,
4600            // Whether another descriptor names this one's description
4601            // (`f_dupfd_query`, which takes an `O_PATH` one too): `EBADF` for
4602            // one that is not open.
4603            F_DUPFD_QUERY => {
4604                let other = args.arg2 as i32;
4605                let mine = guest
4606                    .thread_state()
4607                    .with_detfd(fd, |detfd| detfd.open_file_id())
4608                    .ok();
4609                match guest
4610                    .thread_state()
4611                    .with_detfd(other, |detfd| detfd.open_file_id())
4612                {
4613                    Ok(theirs) => Ok(i64::from(Some(theirs) == mine)),
4614                    Err(_) if other < 0 => Err(Errno::EBADF.into()),
4615                    Err(_) => match guest
4616                        .inject(
4617                            syscalls::Fcntl::new()
4618                                .with_fd(other)
4619                                .with_cmd(FcntlCmd::F_GETFD),
4620                        )
4621                        .await
4622                    {
4623                        Ok(_) => Ok(0),
4624                        Err(errno) => Err(errno.into()),
4625                    },
4626                }
4627            }
4628            // A directory the open did not create (`FMODE_CREATED`).
4629            F_CREATED_QUERY => Ok(0),
4630            // `check_fcntl_cmd`: no other command is allowed on `O_PATH`.
4631            _ if state.path_only => Err(Errno::EBADF.into()),
4632            libc::F_SETFL => {
4633                // `setfl` keeps only `SETFL_MASK`.
4634                const SETFL_MASK: i32 =
4635                    libc::O_APPEND | libc::O_NONBLOCK | libc::O_DIRECT | libc::O_NOATIME;
4636                let requested = args.arg2 as i32;
4637                let status = guest
4638                    .thread_state()
4639                    .with_detfd(fd, |detfd| detfd.status_flags())
4640                    .unwrap_or(libc::O_DIRECTORY);
4641                if requested & libc::O_NOATIME != 0
4642                    && status & libc::O_NOATIME == 0
4643                    && !match placeholder_owner_or_capable(&state.directory) {
4644                        Ok(owner) => owner,
4645                        Err(errno) => return Some(Err(errno.into())),
4646                    }
4647                {
4648                    Err(Errno::EPERM.into())
4649                } else if requested & libc::O_DIRECT != 0 {
4650                    Err(Errno::EINVAL.into())
4651                } else {
4652                    let _ = guest.thread_state().with_detfd(fd, |detfd| {
4653                        detfd.set_status_flags((status & !SETFL_MASK) | (requested & SETFL_MASK));
4654                    });
4655                    Ok(0)
4656                }
4657            }
4658            // The owner and signal commands, which act on the description
4659            // alike for every file.
4660            libc::F_SETOWN
4661            | libc::F_GETOWN
4662            | F_SETSIG
4663            | F_GETSIG
4664            | F_SETOWN_EX
4665            | F_GETOWN_EX
4666            | F_GETOWNER_UIDS => return None,
4667            // Record locks on the object would conflict across directories
4668            // (on procfs's root every placeholder is one inode), where Linux's
4669            // inodes are each the directory's own: refused by name (named
4670            // residual).
4671            libc::F_GETLK
4672            | libc::F_SETLK
4673            | libc::F_SETLKW
4674            | libc::F_OFD_GETLK
4675            | libc::F_OFD_SETLK
4676            | libc::F_OFD_SETLKW => {
4677                warn!(
4678                    "[detcore] fcntl({fd}, {command}), a record lock on a /proc directory \
4679                     Detcore serves, is refused: ENOLCK"
4680                );
4681                self.refuse_unserviceable_operation(guest, Sysno::fcntl, Errno::ENOLCK)
4682                    .await
4683            }
4684            // A lease of an invalid type first (`lease_alloc`); then the
4685            // file's owner, or CAP_LEASE in the initial user namespace, which
4686            // no guest in a user namespace has (`vfs_setlease`); then a
4687            // regular file.
4688            libc::F_SETLEASE
4689                if !matches!(
4690                    args.arg2 as libc::c_int,
4691                    libc::F_RDLCK | libc::F_WRLCK | libc::F_UNLCK
4692                ) =>
4693            {
4694                Err(Errno::EINVAL.into())
4695            }
4696            libc::F_SETLEASE => match placeholder_lease_owner(&state.directory) {
4697                Ok(true) => Err(Errno::EINVAL.into()),
4698                Ok(false) => Err(Errno::EACCES.into()),
4699                Err(errno) => Err(errno.into()),
4700            },
4701            libc::F_GETLEASE => Ok(i64::from(libc::F_UNLCK)),
4702            // Adding seals needs a writable descriptor, before Linux looks
4703            // for seals the file cannot have.
4704            libc::F_ADD_SEALS => Err(Errno::EPERM.into()),
4705            libc::F_GET_SEALS => Err(Errno::EINVAL.into()),
4706            // Not a pipe (`get_pipe_info`).
4707            libc::F_SETPIPE_SZ | libc::F_GETPIPE_SZ => Err(Errno::EBADF.into()),
4708            // dnotify takes the directory; Detcore sends no event.
4709            libc::F_NOTIFY => Ok(0),
4710            F_GET_RW_HINT => {
4711                let hint = crate::tool_global::placeholder_write_hint(
4712                    guest,
4713                    state.directory.lock_key(),
4714                    None,
4715                )
4716                .await;
4717                match AddrMut::<u64>::from_raw(args.arg2) {
4718                    Some(address) => guest
4719                        .memory()
4720                        .write_value(address, &hint)
4721                        .map(|()| 0)
4722                        .map_err(Into::into),
4723                    None => Err(Errno::EFAULT.into()),
4724                }
4725            }
4726            F_SET_RW_HINT => {
4727                match placeholder_owner_or_capable(&state.directory) {
4728                    Ok(true) => {}
4729                    Ok(false) => return Some(Err(Errno::EPERM.into())),
4730                    Err(errno) => return Some(Err(errno.into())),
4731                }
4732                match reverie::syscalls::Addr::<u64>::from_raw(args.arg2)
4733                    .map(|hint| guest.memory().read_value(hint))
4734                {
4735                    Some(Ok(hint)) if hint <= RWH_WRITE_LIFE_EXTREME => {
4736                        crate::tool_global::placeholder_write_hint(
4737                            guest,
4738                            state.directory.lock_key(),
4739                            Some(hint),
4740                        )
4741                        .await;
4742                        Ok(0)
4743                    }
4744                    Some(Ok(_)) => Err(Errno::EINVAL.into()),
4745                    _ => Err(Errno::EFAULT.into()),
4746                }
4747            }
4748            _ => Err(Errno::EINVAL.into()),
4749        })
4750    }
4751
4752    /// `ioctl` through placeholder descriptor `fd`
4753    /// (see [`Self::answer_placeholder_call`]), as `do_vfs_ioctl` answers it
4754    /// for the directory, or `None` for a command that reaches the descriptor
4755    /// (v6.17 `fs/ioctl.c`):
4756    /// - `FIOCLEX` and `FIONCLEX` reach the descriptor's table slot;
4757    /// - `FIONBIO` reads its flag (`EFAULT`) and sets or clears
4758    ///   `O_NONBLOCK` in the description's status flags; `FIOASYNC` reads its
4759    ///   flag, then a change of the description's `O_ASYNC` needs a `fasync`
4760    ///   the directory has not (`ENOTTY`), and no change is 0;
4761    /// - `FIGETBSZ` procfs's 1024; `FIOQSIZE` 0;
4762    /// - `FIFREEZE` and `FITHAW` need `CAP_SYS_ADMIN` in procfs's user
4763    ///   namespace, the guest's (`EPERM`), then procfs cannot freeze
4764    ///   (`EOPNOTSUPP`) and is not frozen (`thaw_super`'s `EINVAL`);
4765    ///   `FS_IOC_FIEMAP` `EOPNOTSUPP`;
4766    /// - `FICLONE` and `FICLONERANGE` (whose argument is read first): the
4767    ///   source descriptor (`EBADF`), then another filesystem's (`EXDEV`),
4768    ///   then a directory (`EISDIR`); `FIDEDUPERANGE`: its destination count
4769    ///   (`EFAULT`), a request past a page (`ENOMEM`), the request
4770    ///   (`EFAULT`), the reserved fields (`EINVAL`), then this directory
4771    ///   (`EISDIR`);
4772    /// - `FS_IOC_SETFLAGS` reads its argument (`EFAULT`); it and every other
4773    ///   command, `FS_IOC_GETFLAGS` among them, are `ENOTTY`: procfs has no
4774    ///   `fileattr_get` and directories no `unlocked_ioctl`.
4775    async fn placeholder_ioctl<G: Guest<Self>>(
4776        &self,
4777        guest: &mut G,
4778        fd: RawFd,
4779        command: u32,
4780        argument: usize,
4781    ) -> Option<Result<i64, Errno>> {
4782        const FIONBIO: usize = 0x5421;
4783        const FIOASYNC: usize = 0x5452;
4784        const FIGETBSZ: usize = 2;
4785        const FIOQSIZE: usize = 0x5460;
4786        const FIFREEZE: usize = 0xc004_5877;
4787        const FITHAW: usize = 0xc004_5878;
4788        const FS_IOC_FIEMAP: usize = 0xc020_660b;
4789        const FICLONE: usize = 0x4004_9409;
4790        const FICLONERANGE: usize = 0x4020_940d;
4791        const FIDEDUPERANGE: usize = 0xc018_9436;
4792        const FS_IOC_SETFLAGS: usize = 0x4008_6602;
4793        const CAP_SYS_ADMIN: u32 = 21;
4794        let flag = |guest: &G| -> Result<bool, Errno> {
4795            let address =
4796                reverie::syscalls::Addr::<libc::c_int>::from_raw(argument).ok_or(Errno::EFAULT)?;
4797            guest
4798                .memory()
4799                .read_value(address)
4800                .map(|value| value != 0)
4801                .map_err(|_| Errno::EFAULT)
4802        };
4803        Some(match command as usize {
4804            // FIONCLEX and FIOCLEX, on the descriptor's table slot.
4805            0x5450 | 0x5451 => return None,
4806            FIONBIO => flag(guest).and_then(|on| {
4807                guest
4808                    .thread_state()
4809                    .with_detfd(fd, |detfd| {
4810                        let status = detfd.status_flags();
4811                        detfd.set_status_flags(if on {
4812                            status | libc::O_NONBLOCK
4813                        } else {
4814                            status & !libc::O_NONBLOCK
4815                        });
4816                    })
4817                    .map(|()| 0)
4818            }),
4819            FIOASYNC => flag(guest).and_then(|on| {
4820                let asynchronous = guest
4821                    .thread_state()
4822                    .with_detfd(fd, |detfd| detfd.status_flags() & libc::O_ASYNC != 0)
4823                    .unwrap_or(false);
4824                if on == asynchronous {
4825                    Ok(0)
4826                } else {
4827                    Err(Errno::ENOTTY)
4828                }
4829            }),
4830            FIGETBSZ => match AddrMut::<libc::c_int>::from_raw(argument) {
4831                Some(address) => guest
4832                    .memory()
4833                    .write_value(address, &(procfs_inode::PROC_BLOCK_SIZE as libc::c_int))
4834                    .map(|()| 0),
4835                None => Err(Errno::EFAULT),
4836            },
4837            // The bytes the directory uses: none.
4838            FIOQSIZE => match AddrMut::<libc::loff_t>::from_raw(argument) {
4839                Some(address) => guest.memory().write_value(address, &0).map(|()| 0),
4840                None => Err(Errno::EFAULT),
4841            },
4842            FIFREEZE | FITHAW => {
4843                let effective = match Self::inject_capget(
4844                    guest,
4845                    super::rendered_procfs::CapabilitySet::Effective,
4846                )
4847                .await
4848                {
4849                    Ok(effective) => effective.unwrap_or(0),
4850                    Err(_) => 0,
4851                };
4852                if effective & (1 << CAP_SYS_ADMIN) == 0 {
4853                    Err(Errno::EPERM)
4854                } else if command as usize == FIFREEZE {
4855                    Err(Errno::EOPNOTSUPP)
4856                } else {
4857                    Err(Errno::EINVAL)
4858                }
4859            }
4860            FS_IOC_FIEMAP => Err(Errno::EOPNOTSUPP),
4861            FICLONE => self.placeholder_clone_answer(guest, argument as i32).await,
4862            // The whole `struct file_clone_range` is read first.
4863            FICLONERANGE => match reverie::syscalls::Addr::<[u64; 4]>::from_raw(argument)
4864                .map(|address| guest.memory().read_value(address))
4865            {
4866                Some(Ok(range)) => self.placeholder_clone_answer(guest, range[0] as i32).await,
4867                _ => Err(Errno::EFAULT),
4868            },
4869            FIDEDUPERANGE => {
4870                // `struct file_dedupe_range`: src_offset, src_length,
4871                // dest_count (u16), reserved1 (u16), reserved2 (u32), then
4872                // dest_count 32-byte `struct file_dedupe_range_info`.
4873                let count = (argument != 0)
4874                    .then(|| reverie::syscalls::Addr::<u16>::from_raw(argument.wrapping_add(16)))
4875                    .flatten()
4876                    .ok_or(Errno::EFAULT)
4877                    .and_then(|address| {
4878                        guest
4879                            .memory()
4880                            .read_value(address)
4881                            .map_err(|_| Errno::EFAULT)
4882                    });
4883                match count {
4884                    Err(errno) => Err(errno),
